## Mandate

Give v5 the operator's hand at Pascal's table exactly as v4 4.11.0 deals it:
a definition carrying `intervention` / `intervene` loads with v4's verdict
and v4's rejection bytes, and the published JSON Schema the Workbench and
`edit_custom_tool` read is the pin's 66,469 bytes; every run result carries
the random draw it used, what the draw and the value could have been, the
prompt as rendered this run and the row the table would have picked, in v4's
key order on every wire that spreads the result; a run reached by a model's
`run_custom` or a trigger phrase on an intervenable tool is dealt, then HELD
between the deal and the effects — nothing applied, nothing posted, no bytes
drawn again, no model called — until the operator previews, re-asks, deals,
lets it stand or sweeps it away through the four actions on
`/api/v1/chats/{id}/custom-tools`, or presses Stop; a hold outlives the
dialog (server state, oldest first, first resolve wins, a late resolve a
409), is announced on the `customToolInterventions` topic on add and on
settle, and waits with NO timeout; a deal that changed nothing records
nothing, a deal that changed something writes `pascalMeta.intervention` LAST
with what was first dealt and which fields moved; a veto posts nothing and
tells a plain character `The tool did not run.` and a transparent one `The
table is closed for the moment; this tool did not run. Carry on without it.`;
the operator's own popup run, an autonomous room and a switched-off setting
never hold, each with v4's DEBUG reason; and the Workbench library says which
tools hold. You land the Rust half of the THREE-WAY grammar twin and the
round's ONE regen of the definition corpus (copied byte-equal to the SPA),
the `InterventionWire` contract under ITS names so P4.D346 authors blind, and
contract T2's `intervention` key under v4's order. You do NOT touch
`api/settings.rs`, the Almanack trio or `tools/help.rs` (P4.D356);
`db/chat_settings.rs`, `fresh_schema.json`, the provisioning seed, the
ensures, `generators/qtap-export.schema.json` (P4.D341); `turn_control.rs`,
`tool_execution.rs`, `trigger_phrase_sites.rs` beyond the fenced two-line
pass-through item 9 names, `orchestrator.rs`, `spine.rs` (P4.D348);
`tool_build.rs`, `tools/executor.rs` beyond one field line, the `effect` key
(P4.D343); `tools/definitions/data.rs` (P4.D350); anything under `apps/web/`
except the two byte copies you own (P4.D346); `help/`, `docs/v4/` (P4.D360).
A departure from an `InterventionWire` / T1 / T2 / `RealtimeTopicAppend` /
`TurnStopReach` name is a STOP-and-flag.

## Survey-verified starting points (2026-10-29, at `236c04e35`)

Measured today from the pin worktree and v5 `main` `f6cdceedd` (core
0.0.1379, host 0.0.217, web 0.0.251, SPA 0.5.907). The long forms are the
survey's §1 / §2 / §4 / §5
(`work-orders/surveys/2026-10-29-setupphase-pascal-intervention.md`);
restated here is what the lane needs in hand, with the corrections the
re-measurement found (§L).

### A. The format — v4 `lib/pascal/custom-tool.types.ts` → v5 `pascal/custom_tool_types.rs`

- `:248-254` `CustomToolParameterSchema.intervene: z.boolean().optional()
  .describe(…)` — the `.describe()` bytes: `Operator intervention only: false
  hides this parameter from the hold dialog. Absent or true shows it. Ignored
  unless the tool's intervention block is enabled.` `:997-1003`
  `CustomToolEffectSchema.intervene` — the same with `this effect`. These
  `.describe()`s reach no model-facing path in v4 (the published JSON Schema
  carries DIFFERENT text — §I); v5 keeps no Zod descriptions, so they are
  recorded, not ported.
- `:1303-1315` `INTERVENTION_FIELD_KEYS = ['roll','value','llmPrompt',
  'llmAnswer','outcome','message','state','chipLabel','title','visibility',
  'announcer'] as const` — the ORDER is load-bearing (`interventionEligibility`
  walks it).
- `:1328-1343` `CustomToolInterventionSchema = z.strictObject({ enabled:
  z.boolean(), fields: z.strictObject({<each key>: z.boolean().optional()})
  .optional() })`. Consequences the corpus must pin: an EMPTY block `{}` is
  refused (`enabled` required — v4's `custom-tool-definition.test.ts` +8
  "rejects an empty block as ambiguous"); `enabled: null` refused (boolean,
  not nullable); an unknown `fields` key is the strict `Unrecognized key:
  "<k>"` issue at path `intervention.fields`; flags on a non-intervenable
  tool and keys naming something the tool lacks (`llmAnswer` with no `llm`)
  are ACCEPTED and inert.
- `:1415-1419` `QtapCustomToolSchema.intervention: CustomToolInterventionSchema
  .optional()` — placed AFTER `announceAsCharacter`, BEFORE `parameters`.
  The known top-level key count goes 17 → 18.
- **v5 today:** `CustomToolParameter` `:572-588`, its known-keys list
  `:3093` `&["type", "default", "description", "min", "max"]`;
  `CustomToolEffect` `:1319-1325`, its list `:3775` `&["when", "target",
  "value", "wardrobe", "inventory", "log"]`; `QtapCustomTool` `:1668-1730`
  with `announce_as_character: Option<bool>` `:1708-1711`, parsed at `:4090`
  by `opt_bool("announceAsCharacter", &mut issues)` and placed at `:4259`;
  strictness is hand-rolled `unrecognized_keys(obj, known)` `:1929` emitting
  `Unrecognized key: "<k>"` / `Unrecognized keys: …` `:1943-1945`;
  `KNOWN_TOP_LEVEL_KEYS: [&str; 17]` `:5399-5417` (`"announceAsCharacter"`
  `:5411`), consumed by `collect_unknown_keys` `:5425-5438`. **The red:** a
  file carrying `intervention` is REFUSED at load today (`Unrecognized key:
  "intervention"`), and a parameter / effect carrying `intervene` likewise.

### B. The engine — v4 `lib/pascal/custom-tools.ts` → v5 `pascal/custom_tools.rs`

- Types: `RecordedDraw` `:752` (`{form:'range', unit}` | `{form:'dice',
  faces}`); `RunSpan {min, max, minExclusive?, maxExclusive?}` `:760-766`;
  `EffectPin {mode:'auto'|'on'|'off', value?, items?, log?}` `:768-779`;
  `RunPins {roll?, dice?, value?, llmAnswer?: {ok, output}, outcomeIndex?,
  message?, state?, chipLabel?, effects?: Record<number, EffectPin>}` `:784-805`.
- `CustomToolRunResult` gains, after `effects?`: `draw: RecordedDraw` `:842`,
  `rollSpan` `:844`, `valueSpan` `:846`, `llmPromptRendered?` `:852`,
  `tableOutcomeIndex?` `:854`. The emitted literal `:2355-2368` spreads
  `…effects, draw, rollSpan, valueSpan, …llmPromptRendered,
  …tableOutcomeIndex` — **this is the wire / oracle key order** (the chat
  POST `result`, the Workbench preview, the execution oracle's `out`).
- `cryptoUnit()` `:997-1002`: the SAME 6-byte / 2^48 math as before,
  returning the unit fraction; the scaling moved into `rollRange`.
- `rollRange(definition, range, params, state, inventory, unitDraw?, pins?)`
  `:1011-1047` → `{raw, value, unit, rollSpan, valueSpan}`: `const unit =
  unitDraw ?? cryptoUnit()` is taken ALWAYS (`:1037`) — **on a degenerate
  `min === max` range v4 now draws 6 bytes and discards the fraction** (at
  `322a5488e` it drew nothing); `raw = pins?.roll ?? (min === max ? min :
  min + unit * (max - min))`; `value = raw * multiplier + offset`, rounded
  if `range.round`, THEN `if (pins?.value !== undefined) value = pins.value`;
  the non-finite check stays; `rollSpan = min === max ? {min, max} : {min,
  max, maxExclusive: true}`; `valueSpan = transformSpan(rollSpan, multiplier,
  offset, range.round === true)`.
- `transformSpan` `:1056-1072`: `low = min*m+o`, `high = max*m+o`; `m === 0`
  → `{min: o, max: o}` (no flags); `m < 0` → `{min: high, max: low,
  minExclusive: true if span.maxExclusive}`; else `{min: low, max: high,
  maxExclusive: true if span.maxExclusive}`; if `round` → `{min:
  Math.round(out.min), max: out.maxExclusive ? Math.ceil(out.max - 0.5) :
  Math.round(out.max)}` with BOTH flags dropped (a swapped span's
  `minExclusive` too — port faithfully; record `ROUND_DROPS_MIN_EXCLUSIVE`
  only if the corpus shows a row where it names an unreachable value).
  `diceSpan(n) = {min: count + modifier, max: count*sides + modifier}`
  `:1074-1076`. JS `Math.round` → v5 `js_round` (`custom_tools.rs:415`).
- `resolveEffects(definition, subjects, pins?)` `:1634-1700`: per index
  `pin.mode === 'off'` → `skipped(index, 'switched off for this run')`;
  `mode !== 'on'` → the `when` test as before (`'condition did not hold'` /
  `` `condition could not be evaluated: ${msg}` ``); `mode === 'on'` skips
  the test; `literalItems = pin.items.map(trim).filter(non-empty)` replaces
  a wardrobe / inventory gesture's `items`, passed as `literal = true` to the
  gesture resolvers (no `renderTemplate` per item); a log line is `pin.log ??
  renderTemplate(effect.log)` then trimmed (`'log line rendered empty'`
  still applies); a write with `pin.value !== undefined` returns `{index,
  target, value: pin.value}` BEFORE the expression path. The existing
  `Custom tool effect skipped` DEBUG fires with the new reason.
- `poseLlmConsult(definition, prompt, invoke)` `:2043-2053`: throws
  `CustomToolRunError(`${name}: this tool consults no model`)` without
  `llm`; else `resolveLlmConsult(name, definition.llm, prompt, invoke)` —
  the existing fail-soft resolver (its WARN `Custom tool LLM consult failed`
  at `:2007` fires as today; v5 `resolve_llm_consult` `:2741` is private —
  `pose_llm_consult` is its one new `pub` door).
- `drawRoll(…, replay?: {draw?, pins?})` `:2099-2137`: dice — `faces =
  pins?.dice ?? (draw.form === 'dice' ? draw.faces : undefined)`; `faces ?
  diceFromFaces(…) : rollNotation(parsed)`; `draw: {form:'dice', faces:
  [...results]}`; `rollSpan = valueSpan = diceSpan(parsed)`. Range — `unit =
  draw.form === 'range' ? draw.unit : undefined` → `rollRange(…, unit,
  pins)`; `draw: {form:'range', unit: used}`.
- `diceFromFaces(toolName, notation, faces)` `:2140-2150`: `faces.length !==
  count` → `CustomToolRunError(`${toolName}: ${faces.length} dice given for
  ${count}d${sides}`)`; each face `Math.min(sides, Math.max(1,
  Math.round(face)))`; `subtotal`; `total = subtotal + modifier`.
- `executeCustomTool(definition, supplied, overrides?)` `:2172-2368` gains
  `draw?` (`:2202`), `consult?` (`:2207`), `pins?` (`:2209`). Order:
  `resolveParams`; `if (pins?.outcomeIndex !== undefined &&
  !definition.outcomes[pins.outcomeIndex]) throw CustomToolRunError(`${name}:
  there is no outcome row ${pins.outcomeIndex}`)` (`:2217`); `prepareRoll`;
  `drawRoll`; the consult closure (`:2260-2264`): `pins.llmAnswer` → `{ok,
  output, prompt}` (NO provider / model / reason keys), else
  `overrides.consult` VERBATIM (its old `prompt` kept — that is how
  `staleAnswer` is detected), else `resolveLlmConsult`; both run orders set
  `llmPromptRendered` to the prompt rendered THIS run, `outcomeIndex =
  pins?.outcomeIndex ?? tablePick`, `tableOutcomeIndex = tablePick` only when
  pinned (`:2272-2297`); `finalState = pins?.state ?? outcome.state` feeds
  `winning.state` (effects test the pinned verdict) and `result.state`;
  `message = pins?.message ?? renderTemplate(…)` (literal — no placeholders
  re-rendered); `chipLabel = pins?.chipLabel ?? render(…)` only when
  declared; `resolveEffects(definition, effectSubjects, pins?.effects)`.
- **v5 today:** `CustomToolRunError(pub String)` `:49`; `LlmConsultResult`
  `:198`; `CustomToolRunResult` `:253-284` ENDS at `pub effects:
  Option<Vec<ResolvedEffect>>` `:283` — no draw record, no spans, no
  `llm_prompt_rendered` / `table_outcome_index`; `crypto_uniform(min, max,
  rng)` `:515-523` scales INSIDE; `roll_range(…, rng) -> Result<(f64, f64)>`
  `:526` short-circuits a degenerate range WITHOUT drawing; `resolve_effects
  (definition, subjects)` `:1801` takes no pins; `resolve_wardrobe_gesture`
  `:1943` / `resolve_inventory_gesture` `:1989` have no `literal` flag;
  `execute_custom_tool` `:2867-2900` takes TWELVE positionals (the last,
  `inventory: Option<&dyn InventoryContext>`, is P4.D329's R-Q); the
  dealt-ahead DEBUG `:3025`; `pick_outcome` `:3127`. The result is projected
  to the wire by `api/custom_tools.rs::run_result_to_value` `:1189-1254`
  (`…llm, chipLabel, effects` — nothing after).

### C. The hold machinery — NEW `lib/pascal/intervention/` → NEW `pascal/intervention/`

- **`registry.ts`** (213 lines): `KEEP_ALIVE_MS = 15_000` `:35` (R-M — NOT
  declared in v5); `SETTLED_MEMORY = 200` `:38`; `InterventionDecision =
  {kind:'deal', overrides, run: RecomputedRun, reasks} | {kind:'as-rolled'}
  | {kind:'veto', cause:'dialog'|'stop'}` `:41-45`; `PendingIntervention {id,
  chatId, userId, createdAt, entrance:'llm'|'trigger', characterId,
  characterName, triggerPhrase?, definition, definitionTier,
  definitionMountId, snapshot, draw, baseline, consults: LlmConsultResult[]}`
  `:47-66`; `processSingleton('pascal.interventionRegistry')` `{byId: Map,
  settled: string[]}` `:78-81`; `hold(pending, {signal?, keepAlive?}) → {id:
  randomUUID(), decision: Promise}` `:93-150` — registers, (the keepAlive
  interval — R-M), an already-aborted signal settles via
  `queueMicrotask(onAbort)` so the add publishes BEFORE the removal, logs
  INFO `Custom tool run held for the operator`, publishes
  `customToolInterventions` for the chat; `settleIntervention(id, decision) →
  'resolved' | 'unknown' | 'settled'` `:158-180` — first wins, the `settled`
  ring trimmed to 200, WARN `Custom tool hold resolve refused` on a miss,
  INFO `Custom tool hold resolved` (`cause` = the veto's cause else
  `'dialog'`), publish again; `interventionStatus(id)` `:183`;
  `getIntervention(id)` `:189`; `listInterventions(chatId)` oldest-first by
  `createdAt` `:194-199` (a STABLE sort — ties keep insertion order);
  `addConsult(id, consult) → boolean` `:202`;
  `resetInterventionRegistryForTests` `:210`.
- **`eligibility.ts`** (75): `isIntervenable(def) = def.intervention?.enabled
  === true` `:19`; `interventionEligibility(def, rollForm) → {fields, params,
  effects}` `:44-75`: `applicable` in `INTERVENTION_FIELD_KEYS` order —
  `value` only for `'range'`, `llmPrompt` / `llmAnswer` only with `llm`,
  `chipLabel` only when declared, the rest always; `fields = keys.filter
  (applicable && flags[key] !== false)`; `params` = declared names whose
  `intervene !== false` (declaration order); `effects` = indices whose
  `intervene !== false`.
- **`overrides.ts`** (131): `EffectPinSchema = strictObject{mode:
  enum(auto|on|off), value?: number().finite() | string | boolean, items?:
  array(string().max(400)).max(16), log?: string().max(MAX_LLM_OUTPUT_CEILING
  + 4000)}` `:34-39`; `InterventionOverridesSchema = strictObject{params?:
  record(string, Literal), roll?: finite, dice?: array(int).min(1)
  .max(MAX_DICE_COUNT), value?: finite, llmPrompt?: string().min(1)
  .max(MAX_LLM_PROMPT_LENGTH), llmAnswer?: strictObject{ok: boolean, output:
  string().max(MAX_LLM_OUTPUT_CEILING)}, outcomeIndex?: int().min(0),
  message?: string().min(1).max(ceiling + 4000), state?: OutcomeStateSchema,
  chipLabel?: min(1).max(MAX_CHIP_LABEL_LENGTH), title?: min(1)
  .max(MAX_TITLE_LENGTH), visibility?: VisibilitySchema, announcer?:
  enum(pascal|character), effects?: record(string().regex(/^\d+$/, 'effect
  keys are indices'), EffectPinSchema)}` `:41-57` (read the four `MAX_*`
  constants' values at the pin from `custom-tool.types.ts` — v5 already
  carries their twins for the format); `InterventionDecisionSchema =
  discriminatedUnion('kind', [strictObject{kind:'deal', overrides},
  strictObject{kind:'as-rolled'}, strictObject{kind:'veto'}])` `:63-67`;
  `checkOverrides(def, {rollForm, diceCount?}, overrides) → string | null`
  `:82-131`, the refusal sentences IN THIS CHECK ORDER: `` `parameter
  "${name}" is not open to intervention` ``; `the roll is not open to
  intervention` (roll OR dice given while `roll` hidden); `a dice roll is set
  by its faces, not a number` (`roll` on the dice form); `this roll is not
  dice` (`dice` on the range form); `` `this roll takes ${diceCount} dice,
  not ${n}` ``; then for the pairs `[value→value, llmPrompt, llmAnswer,
  outcomeIndex→outcome, message, state, chipLabel, title, visibility,
  announcer]`: `` `the ${field} is not open to intervention` ``; `` `there is
  no outcome row ${i}` ``; `` `effect ${i} is not open to intervention` ``.
- **`recompute.ts`** (269): `RunSnapshot {params: Record|null, private:
  boolean|undefined, metadata, progress, now, character: string|null, state,
  inventory}` `:32-43`; `RunPresentation {title, visibility, announcer}`;
  `RecomputedRun {result, presentation, staleAnswer, promptNow?,
  clampedParams, warnings}` `:65-76`; `pinsFromOverrides` `:79-93` (effects
  re-keyed `Number(key)`; each key present only when the override is);
  `presentationFor(def, visibility, {title?, announcer?}) = {title:
  overrides.title ?? displayTitle(def), visibility, announcer:
  overrides.announcer ?? (def.announceAsCharacter === true ? 'character' :
  'pascal')}` `:96-106`; `recomputeRun(input)` `:112-179`: `supplied =
  {...snapshot.params, ...overrides.params}`; `privacy = overrides.visibility
  !== undefined ? visibility === 'whisper' : snapshot.private`;
  `executeCustomTool` with the snapshot's metadata / progress / now /
  character / state / inventory + `draw`, `consult`, `pins`; `clampedParams`
  = override names whose resolved value `!==` the given one; `promptNow =
  def.llm ? (overrides.llmPrompt ?? result.llmPromptRendered) : undefined`;
  `staleAnswer = llm && overrides.llmAnswer === undefined && consult !==
  undefined && promptNow !== undefined && promptNow !== consult.prompt`;
  warnings in order: `` `The table would have dealt row ${tableOutcomeIndex
  + 1} here; the odds are shown to the model, so a forced row may look out
  of place.` `` (only when `revealOdds !== false`), `The message is pinned,
  but a different outcome row now wins; check that the words still fit it.`
  (only with `dealtOutcomeIndex`), `` `The pinned draw lies outside what the
  table could deal (${describeSpan(rollSpan)}).` ``, `` `The pinned value
  lies outside what the modifiers could reach (${describeSpan(valueSpan)}).`
  ``, `The consult's answer was given to a different prompt. Re-ask, or deal
  it as it stands.`; `summarizeEffects` `:190-200` (`target = authored.target
  ?? (wardrobe ? 'wardrobe' : inventory ? 'inventory' : 'log')`, `next` =
  value / wardrobe.items / inventory.items / log; `fired: false` with no
  `next` for a skipped one); `RunFacts` + `factsOf` `:203-236` (key order
  `params, raw, diceRolls?, value, outcomeIndex, state, message, chipLabel?,
  toolTitle, visibility, announcer, llm?: {ok, output, prompt}, effects?`);
  `changedFields(dealt, final)` `:246-269` — keys `params.<name>` (the UNION
  of both param key sets, `JSON.stringify`-compared), `roll` (raw OR
  diceRolls differ), `value`, `llmAnswer` (`{ok, output}` JSON-compared),
  `outcome`, `message`, `state`, `chipLabel`, `title`, `visibility`,
  `announcer`, `effects.<index>` (absent after, or `fired` / `next` differ),
  in THAT order. `same(a, b)` is `JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b)` —
  a BYTE compare (memory note `serde-json-map-eq-ignores-order`).
- **`veto-result.ts`** (23): `VETO_RESULT_PLAIN = 'The tool did not run.'`;
  `VETO_RESULT_TRANSPARENT = 'The table is closed for the moment; this tool
  did not run. Carry on without it.'`; `vetoResultFor(transparent)`.
- **`view.ts`** (100, types only — read whole; it IS the wire):
  `InterventionEffectView {index, kind: write|wardrobe|inventory|log, label,
  fired, skipped?, value?, items?, log?}`; `InterventionRunView {params,
  rollForm, notation?, raw, diceRolls?, diceBreakdown, rollSpan, value,
  valueSpan, outcomeIndex, tableOutcomeIndex?, state, message, chipLabel?,
  title, visibility, announcer, llm?: {ok, output, prompt}, effects}`;
  `InterventionPreview {run, staleAnswer, promptNow?, clampedParams,
  warnings}`; `InterventionHoldView {id, tool, title, characterName,
  entrance, triggerPhrase?, createdAt, eligible: {fields, params, effects},
  hasCharacter, definition: {parameters, outcomes: [{when, state, message,
  brief?}], effects, revealOdds, hasLlm, diceSides?}, baseline:
  InterventionPreview, consults: [{ok, output, prompt}]}`.
- **`spans.ts`** (36, `8ae8a127c`): `withinSpan(n, span)` honouring exclusive
  ends; `describeSpan`: `` `exactly ${min}` `` when `min === max` and neither
  exclusive; `` `more than ${min}, up to ${maxExclusive ? '(not including) '
  + max : max}` `` when `minExclusive`; `` `${min} up to (not including)
  ${max}` `` when `maxExclusive`; else `` `${min} to ${max}` ``; numbers via
  `Number.isInteger(n) ? String(n) : String(Number(n.toPrecision(4)))` —
  v5 twins exist: `pascal/js_value.rs` `number_to_string` `:207` and
  `to_precision(x, p)` `:260` (ECMA-262 20.1.3.5); the twin is
  `number_to_string(parse(to_precision(n, 4)))` for a non-integer.
- **v5 today:** NO-COUNTERPART. The process-global idiom to copy is
  `services/turn_control.rs:88 registry()` (a `Mutex` + `Arc<Entry>`); the
  decision channel is a `tokio::sync::oneshot` (a second resolve finds the
  entry gone → `'settled'`); `OnceLock` / `LazyLock` for the singleton.

### D. The run service — v4 `lib/pascal/run-service.ts` → v5 `pascal/run_service.rs`

- `RunCustomToolParams.mayHold: boolean` `:103`; `RunCustomToolOutcome |
  {ok: 'vetoed'}` `:125`; the chat is now STORED (`let chat = params.chat ??
  null; chat = chat ?? await findById(chatId)` `:147-149`) so the autonomous
  check reuses it; the hold block `:200-220` AFTER execute, BEFORE effects:
  `presentation = presentationFor(def, result.visibility)`; `if (await
  mayHoldRun(params, chat))` → `holdForOperator(params, {characterName,
  result, snapshot: {params: params.parameters ?? null, private:
  params.private, metadata, progress, now: nowMs, character: characterName,
  state: cascade?.merged ?? {}, inventory}})`; `veto` → `return {ok:
  'vetoed'}`; `deal` → `result = held.run.result; presentation =
  held.run.presentation; intervention = held.record`. Downstream: `toolTitle
  = presentation.title` (was `displayTitle`), `announcerCharacterId =
  presentation.announcer === 'character' ? characterId : null`, and
  `pascalMeta: {…, …(intervention ? {intervention} : {})}` appended LAST
  (after `callerParticipantId`).
- `mayHoldRun(params, chat)` `:306-337`: `!isIntervenable` → `false`
  SILENTLY; then each skip logs DEBUG `Custom tool run not held` `{context:
  'pascal.run-service', chatId, tool, reason}` with the reasons, in order:
  `this entrance never holds (the operator's own run)` (`!params.mayHold`),
  `running in the job child` (`QUILLTAP_JOB_CHILD === '1'`), `an autonomous
  room` (`chat?.chatType === 'autonomous'`), `custom tools are off`
  (`settings?.customTools === false`), `the operator switched intervention
  off` (`settings?.customToolIntervention === false`); the settings read is
  `chatSettings.findByUserId(userId)` — a `safeQuery` FALLBACK (null on a
  failed row read, with the repository's own ERROR); a THROW → DEBUG `Custom
  tool hold: chat settings unreadable, holding by default` `{context, chatId,
  error}` and HOLD.
- `holdForOperator` `:345-410`: `hold({chatId, userId, entrance: invokedBy
  === 'trigger' ? 'trigger' : 'llm', characterId, characterName,
  …triggerPhrase?, definition, definitionTier: entry.tier,
  definitionMountId: entry.mountPointId, snapshot, draw: result.draw,
  baseline: result, consults: result.llm ? [result.llm] : []}, {signal:
  currentTurnStopSignal(), keepAlive: currentTurnKeepAlive()})`; veto → INFO
  `Custom tool run set aside by the operator` `{context, chatId, tool,
  invokedBy, cause}` → `{kind:'veto'}`; `as-rolled` → `{kind:'as-rolled'}`;
  deal → `before = factsOf(def, result, presentationFor(def,
  result.visibility))`, `after = factsOf(def, run.result, run.presentation)`,
  `changed = changedFields(before, after)`, INFO `Custom tool run dealt by the
  operator` `{context, chatId, tool, changed, reasks, staleAnswer}`,
  **`changed.length === 0` → `{kind:'as-rolled'}` (records nothing)**, else
  `{kind:'deal', run, record: {resolvedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  decision: 'deal', changed, dealt: before, …(reasks > 0 ? {reasks}),
  …(staleAnswer ? {staleAnswer: true})}}`.
- **v5 today:** `RunCustomToolParams<'a>` `:84-137` (no `may_hold`, no
  `stop`); `RunCustomToolOutcome {Ok, Refused}` `:141-153` (no `Vetoed`);
  `run_custom_tool` `:163`; the chat read lives INSIDE the cascade closure
  (`held_chat = params.chat` `:200`; `find_by_id_or_none(main, chat_id)`
  `:210`) and is NOT retained past it — the hold's autonomous check needs it
  hoisted; the cascade-unavailable DEBUG `:235-247` is the precedent for a
  connection-failure arm; `load_run_inventory` `:253`; `execute_custom_tool`
  `:260-276`; the refused arm `:278-294`; `apply_effects_for_run` `:299-313`;
  `tool_title = display_title(&name, title)` `:323`; `build_pascal_meta(`
  `:327-335` (fn `:446`, assembling `tool, toolTitle, chipLabel?,
  definitionTier, definitionMountId, params, …, invokedBy, triggerPhrase?,
  callerParticipantId?` in v4's order — `intervention` goes AFTER
  `callerParticipantId`); the announcer `:342-346` reads the DEFINITION
  (`announce_as_character == Some(true)`), not a presentation;
  `post_pascal_result` `:348-358`; the `Custom tool dealt` DEBUG `:362-379`
  with `announcedBy`; `Ok(RunCustomToolOutcome::Ok {…})` `:381`.

### E. The entrances

- **v4 `lib/tools/handlers/run-custom-handler.ts`:** `mayHold: true` `:217`;
  `callerKnowsItIsAGame(ctx)` `:104-123`: `chats.findById(chatId)` → the
  caller seat (`participants.find(p.id === callerParticipantId)`)
  `controlledBy === 'user'` → `true`; no `characterId` → `false`;
  `characters.findByIdRaw(characterId)?.systemTransparency === true`; any
  THROW → DEBUG `run_custom veto: caller transparency unreadable, treating as
  plain` `{context, chatId, error}` → `false`. The vetoed arm `:227-238`: NO
  Prospero bubble; INFO `run_custom set aside by the operator` `{context,
  chatId, tool, transparent}`; returns `failure(vetoResultFor(transparent),
  toolName)` — the ORDINARY failure shape (`success: false, error: <veto
  text>`), so `formatRunCustomResults` renders `Custom tool error: The tool
  did not run.`.
- **v4 `lib/pascal/trigger-runner.ts`:** `mayHold: true` `:198`; `outcome.ok
  === 'vetoed'` → DEBUG `Triggered custom tool set aside by the operator`
  `{context, chatId, tool, phrase}` and `continue` `:211-220`.
- **v4 `app/api/v1/chats/[id]/custom-tools/route.ts`:** the operator's
  `?action=run` passes `mayHold: false` `:465` and keeps an unreachable arm
  `vetoed → successResponse({messages: [], result: null})` `:469-472`.
- **v5 today:** `tools/run_custom.rs` `RunCustomToolContext` `:662-697`
  (derives `Debug, Clone, Default`; no turn-control handle); `HANDLER_CONTEXT
  = "run-custom-handler"` `:711`; `failure(error, tool)` `:715`;
  `report_failure` `:756`; the service call `:967-995`; the outcome match
  `:997-1016` (`Ok` / `Refused` / `Err`); the unposted arm `:1019-1032`.
  `pascal/trigger_runner.rs` `RunTriggeredCustomToolsParams` `:77-88`
  (derives `Debug, Clone, Default`); the per-hit loop `for hit in &scan.hits`
  `:240`; the call `:261-287` (`invoked_by: InvokedBy::Trigger`,
  `trigger_phrase: Some(&hit.phrase)`); the `Err` fold `:288-294`; the match
  `:296+` (`Refused` `:297-322`, `Ok {posted: None}` `:323-350`, `Ok`
  `:351`). `api/custom_tools.rs::chat_custom_tool_run` `:635` passes
  `invoked_by: InvokedBy::User` with no `may_hold`. `tools/executor.rs

  `:731-748` and calls `execute_run_custom_tool_with_consult` `:775`. The
  transparency precedent `services/carina_query.rs:1076 resolve_asker_flag`
  is private and NOT reused (R-6 — v4's handler does its own two reads).

### F. Turn control and the keep-alive (R-M)

- v4 `lib/chat/turn-control.ts`: `TurnControlEntry.keepAlive?: () =>
  boolean` `:51`; `runWithTurnControl(chatId, fn, options: {keepAlive?})`
  `:69-82`; `currentTurnStopSignal()` `:112`; `currentTurnKeepAlive()`
  `:120-122`; `stopActiveTurns` `:202`. The ONE keepAlive caller:
  `lib/services/chat-message/turn-stream.ts:60` `{keepAlive: () =>
  safeEnqueue(controller, encodeKeepAlive(encoder))}` — `': keep-alive\n\n'`
  (`streaming.service.ts:882-884`) every 15 s on the SSE response while a
  hold waits.
- v5: `services/turn_control.rs` `TurnControlHandle` `:101` (Debug, Clone);
  `StopSignal` `:105` with `stopped()` `:109` and `async wait()` `:115`
  (resolves at once when already stopped — v4's aborted-signal settle);
  `handle.stop_signal()` `:139`; `is_stopped()` `:144`;
  `register_active_turn` `:226`; `stop_active_turns` `:300`. **`StopSignal::
  wait()` IS the veto hook.** There is NO keep-alive concept: the Salon send
  is a JSON `ChatSend` dispatch the host AWAITS whole on a spawned thread
  (`quilltap-host/src/spine.rs:1460 run_send`, the `std::thread::spawn`
  sites `:2429-2801`), and the Event channel pings ITSELF every 15 s
  (`quilltap-web/src/events.rs:33 KEEP_ALIVE_INTERVAL`). A held run
  therefore holds the `POST …/messages` response open with NO bytes for as
  long as the operator takes — ruling R-M.

### G. The routes — v4 `interventions.ts` (353 lines) → NEW `api/custom_tool_interventions.rs`

- `route.ts:517` GET `withActionDispatch({interventions:
  handleListInterventions}, handleList)`; `:521-526` POST `{run,
  'intervention-preview', 'intervention-reask', 'intervention-resolve'}`. An
  unknown `?action=` → v4's `withActionDispatch` envelope — v5's
  `quilltap-web/src/query.rs` `dispatch_action` / `dispatch_required_action`
  already answer it (`custom_tools_routes.rs:50`, `:72`).
- `HANDLER = 'api.v1.chats.custom-tools.interventions'`; `effectView`
  (`:70-93`: `kind` from the authored effect — `wardrobe` / `inventory` /
  `log !== undefined` / else `write`; `label` = `target ?? 'write'` /
  `` `wardrobe ${action}`.trim() `` / `` `inventory ${action}`.trim() `` /
  `'log'`; a skipped effect `{…base, fired: false, skipped}`); `runView`
  (`:95-117`, the key order in §C's `InterventionRunView`); `previewOf`;
  `holdView` (`:129-164`: `title = presentationFor(def, baseline.visibility)
  .title`, `eligible = interventionEligibility(def, baseline.rollForm)`,
  `hasCharacter = characterId !== null`, `definition.diceSides` from
  `parseDiceNotation(baseline.notation).sides` only on the dice form,
  `definition.revealOdds = revealOdds !== false`, `hasLlm`, `baseline =
  {run, staleAnswer: false, promptNow?: llmPromptRendered, clampedParams:
  [], warnings: []}`, `consults` mapped to `{ok, output, prompt}`);
  `findHold(chatId, id)` (a hold of ANOTHER chat is `null` → 404);
  `refusal(pending, overrides)` = `checkOverrides(def, {rollForm:
  baseline.rollForm, diceCount?: baseline.diceRolls.length}, overrides)`;
  `recompute(pending, overrides)` with `consult = consults[last]`,
  `dealtOutcomeIndex = baseline.outcomeIndex`; bodies through `readBody`: a
  non-JSON body → 400 `The request body is not JSON`, a Zod failure → 400
  `issues.map(message).join('; ')`; `previewSchema = z.object({id:
  z.string().min(1), overrides: InterventionOverridesSchema})` (a NON-strict
  outer object), `resolveSchema = z.object({id, decision})`.
- Handlers: **list** → `{holds}` + DEBUG `Custom tool holds listed`
  `{context, chatId, holds: n}` (`:224`); **preview** → 404 `notFound('Held
  run')` (the envelope message `Held run not found`), 400 on a refusal, 400
  on `CustomToolRunError.message`, DEBUG `Custom tool hold previewed`
  `{context, chatId, holdId, pinned: Object.keys(overrides), staleAnswer}`
  (`:243`) → `{preview}`; **reask** (`b02d902f3`) → 404; `!definition.llm` →
  400 `This tool consults no oracle` (`:267-273`; the eligibility check was
  DELETED — the old `This run's consult is not open to intervention` must
  NOT be ported); `llmAnswer` STRIPPED from the overrides; a refusal → 400;
  `before = recompute`; `prompt = before.promptNow ?? definition.llm.prompt`;
  `consult = poseLlmConsult(def, prompt, buildCustomToolLlmInvoker({userId,
  chatId}))` (💸 a model call); `addConsult` false → 409 `That run has
  already been settled`; DEBUG `Custom tool hold re-asked` `{context, chatId,
  holdId, ok, reasks: consults.length - 1}` (`:288`); `after = recompute` →
  `{preview, consults}`; a non-`CustomToolRunError` throw → WARN `Custom tool
  hold re-ask failed` `{context, chatId, error}` (`:302`) and rethrow (500);
  **resolve** → a missing hold: WARN `Custom tool hold resolve refused`
  `{context, chatId, holdId, status}` (`:320`) then 409 `That run has already
  been settled` if `status === 'settled'` else 404 `Held run not found`;
  `as-rolled` / `veto` settle (`cause: 'dialog'`) → `{resolved: kind}` or
  409; `deal` → refusal 400, recompute (400 on `CustomToolRunError`),
  `reasks = max(0, consults.length - (baseline.llm ? 1 : 0))`, settle →
  `{resolved: 'deal'}` or 409.

### Tier 1 — must land

One commit per unit (`.claude/commands/commit.md`), each with its family
green on YOUR branch bar the rows named red-by-design (counted in the
record); fmt / bumps after the gates (memory note
`keystone-lane-swarm-gotchas`). Items 2–6 compile on `main` (the PRE-START
allowance); items 7–13 need `SPINE`.

     definition.oracle.ndjson` — ANY difference is a sibling's grammar at the

     record each by id and attribute it, memory note
     `attribute-reds-by-stubbing-v4`). Then add your rows (item 2) and
     re-measure: EVERY new accepted row RED on `main`'s parser (`Unrecognized

     differs, the 18th tripwire row RED.

     (own recipe copy, outputs `/tmp/p4d345/`): count the rows whose

     equivalence`, `pascal_workbench_equivalence`** from the pin: the rows
     that gained `draw` / `rollSpan` / `valueSpan` / `llmPromptRendered`; the

     flips); `changed_fields` comparing `effects` by `fired` alone (the

     BEFORE `this roll is not dice` (indistinguishable on the range form — an

## 💸 for the dogfood pass

On a Friday copy at ANY v4 point ≥ `839575d89` (or a fresh instance), after
the union with P4.D346 / P4.D356:

1. Salon, trigger phrase, no `llm`: say the phrase → the dialog `Pascal
   holds: <title>` with `overheard: “…”`; `As rolled` posts the bubble
   unchanged; no badge; the log carries `Custom tool run held for the
   operator` then `Custom tool hold resolved … decision=as-rolled cause=dialog`.
2. 💸 Salon, a character's `run_custom`: the dialog; pin the outcome row →
   `The table would have dealt row …` (revealOdds on); `Deal it` → the bubble
   carries the badge (`Adjusted before it was dealt: outcome row 2 → 1`), the
   model's tool result is the FINAL run only (LLM Inspector).
3. 💸 Veto, plain character: `Set the table aside` → nothing posted; the
   model's next turn received `The tool did not run.`; a `systemTransparency`
   character receives `The table is closed for the moment; …`.
4. Stop while held: the dialog closes, nothing posted, the turn ends; the
   log's `cause=stop` — ONLY if `TurnStopReach` landed (else record H1's WARN
   in the log).
5. Reload with a hold pending: the dialog reappears (server state); a second
   tab shows the same hold; resolving in one makes the other answer 409 `That
   run has already been settled`.
6. Two holds queued (two phrases in one message): oldest first; ` 1 more
   waiting.`.
7. 💸 Re-ask: pin a parameter the prompt quotes → the stale note; `Re-ask`
   spends ONE call (an `llm_logs` `CUSTOM_TOOL_CONSULT` row), `reasks: 1` in
   `pascalMeta.intervention` after `Deal it`.
8. 💸 Hidden consult (`fields: {llmPrompt: false, llmAnswer: false}`): a
   pinned `llmPrompt` POSTed by hand → 400 `the llmPrompt is not open to
   intervention`; Re-ask still works (`b02d902f3`).
9. Spans: a `$param`-bounded range → `The table draws 0 up to (not including)
   10.`; pin a roll outside → the warning; dice `3d6+2` → `5 to 20`.
10. Settings off (P4.D356's card): the same phrase deals as rolled with no
    dialog; the log's `the operator switched intervention off`.
11. Autonomous room with an intervenable tool: deals as rolled (`an
    autonomous room`).
12. **R-M measurement:** hold a run for 10 minutes over HTTP (Docker) and in
    Tauri; the POST survives and the bubble posts on `Deal it`. Record the
    outcome in the lane record's R-M table.
13. `.qtap` export / backup → restore of a chat carrying `pascalMeta
    .intervention`: the record survives byte-equal; a definition file carrying
    `intervention` imports (no `Unrecognized key`).
14. The operator's Run Tool on an intervenable tool: never held (`this
    entrance never holds (the operator's own run)`).

## Rulings made at planning (the planner's calls; the human may overrule before lane start — a STOP item if they do)

Round rulings that govern this lane, by reference: **THE PIN** (§R.1 / §R.2);
**the npm-cache gap** (the Preamble); **R-A** (the custom-tool schema and
the definition corpus are YOURS — the export schema, `fresh_schema.json`,
`migration_indexes.json`, `schema-key-order.json` are the KEYSTONE's; the
tool catalog is P4.D350's); **R-E** (observable shifts, not file splits —
your module split mirrors v4's seven files because each is a unit the pure
family names); **R-M** (below); **R-Z** (not this round's: the owed dogfood
pass, the CLI `memories` family, every v4 filing candidate); the struct-
literal rule (every new field on `RunCustomToolParams` / `RunCustomToolContext`
/ `RunTriggeredCustomToolsParams` is named by every caller — no `..Default`);
**§R.5** (your tag set: §K's lines, each with a capture pin AND a silence
leg; an array-valued key through `<key>Json`); **§R.13** (your families).
**Version bumps** (§R.6): `quilltap-core` +1 per commit touching it;
`quilltap-web` +1 on the commit that moves `custom_tools_routes.rs`; the SPA
+1 on the commits that move `apps/web/public/schemas/` and the fixture copy
(per `commit.md`); `quilltap-harness` NEVER (frozen `0.0.1110`);
`quilltap-sqlite3mc-sys` NEVER; `quilltap-host`, `quilltap-cli`,
`quilltap-tauri` untouched.

- ⚖ **R-M — `HOLD_KEEP_ALIVE_NO_STREAM` (the planner's round ruling;
  recorded here as this lane's).** v4's `keepAlive` ping has no v5
  counterpart by architecture (§F). Record as a deliberate divergence (a
  NAMED both-ways table; the SPA's send `fetch` has no timeout —
  `core-transport.ts` sets none; Tauri `invoke` has none) with dogfood row 12
  as the measurement. The alternative — a dispatch-level keep-alive threaded
  through `spine.rs` / `events.rs` — is P4.D348's host work and is NOT
  ordered this round. If the human overrules, this lane changes nothing; the
  hold's await is already transport-agnostic.
- ⚖ **R-2 — `execute_custom_tool`'s signature: `Option<&ReplayInputs<'_>>`
  as the THIRTEENTH positional** (overturning the previous round's scoped
  R-Q "one more positional, no overrides struct" for THIS addition). Why:
  three replay inputs travel together or not at all (`drawRoll`'s `replay`
  object IS v4's shape), every production caller passes `None`, and fifteen
  positionals would make every future replay edit a fifteen-site change.
  The alternative (three positionals → 15) is faithful to R-Q's letter and
  costs nothing in behaviour. The lane does the struct unless overruled
  before lane start.
- **R-3 — the always-drawn unit: NO divergence; the byte-stream fact
  flipped.** v5 follows v4 (`roll_range` draws the unit unconditionally); the
  execution family's header and the oracle's `:891` comment are rewritten;
  the `bytesConsumed` delta per degenerate row (+6) is recorded. The human
  should know the fact changed; nothing to choose.
- **R-4 — the Stop veto's home: `TurnStopReach` is P4.D348's** (the field on
  `ToolExecutionContext`, the two site param structs, the orchestrator fill)
  — approved by the planner in the lane table. Your three-outcome
  measurement (item 9) decides consumption vs the fenced pass-through vs the
  loud WARN HANDOFF. Never a silent `None`.
- **R-5 — `SettingsArmOwner`: P4.D356 owns the PUT arm, the Almanack trio,
  `help.rs:216`** (the keystone survey's note overridden). You touch none.
- **R-6 — the veto's transparency read is v4's OWN two reads** (`chats.
  findById` + `characters.findByIdRaw`, fallback semantics, the DEBUG on a
  connection failure) — NOT `carina_query.rs:1076 resolve_asker_flag` (no
  visibility change in a file nobody owns this round).
- **R-7 — `pascalMeta.intervention` is an opaque `Option<serde_json::Value>`
  on `PascalMetaIn`** (v4's Zod shape is a non-strict `z.object`; a typed
  twin would REFUSE rows a newer v4 writes). The run service builds the
  record as an ordered `Map` (`resolvedAt, decision, changed, dealt, reasks?,
  staleAnswer?`); the carriers pass the blob (MEASURE: `grep -rn PascalMetaIn
  crates/quilltap-core/src/services` — if backup / `.qtap` export / restore
  never re-parse through it, nothing else moves; record).
- **R-8 — the realtime invalidation prefix** (`ALL_REALTIME_PREFIXES +=
  customTools.all`) is the SPA's — P4.D346. You add the topic, the
  `job_topics` arm and the two publish pins only.
- **R-9 — no Tier R** (§L.5): `cli_differential` run once at close, `N / 0`
  recorded.
- **R-10 — the registry is a process-global `Mutex` + `oneshot`**, never a
  per-engine field: v4's `processSingleton` is the spec, the API and the
  spine run on different runtimes / threads, and a hold must survive the
  SPA's reload (dogfood row 5). `list_interventions` keeps insertion order
  for equal `created_at` (JS's stable sort).

### Contract `InterventionWire` (P4.D345 builds, P4.D346 consumes — BINDING names; the planner's block carries the binding copy)

- **Dispatch verbs (serde tag = camelCase variant):**
  `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionsList","chatId"}` →
  `Response::CustomToolInterventions(Value)` = `{"holds": InterventionHoldView[]}`;
  `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionPreview","chatId","id","overrides"}` →
  `Response::CustomToolInterventionPreview(Value)` = `{"preview": InterventionPreview}`;
  `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionReask","chatId","id","overrides"}` →
  `Response::CustomToolInterventionReask(Value)` = `{"preview", "consults": [{ok, output, prompt}]}`;
  `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionResolve","chatId","id","decision"}` →
  `Response::CustomToolInterventionResolve(Value)` = `{"resolved": "deal" | "as-rolled" | "veto"}`.
- **Errors** (`Response::error(kind, msg)`): 404 `Held run not found`; 409
  `That run has already been settled`; 400 — `This tool consults no oracle`,
  every `checkOverrides` sentence (§C), `CustomToolRunError`'s message (e.g.
  `` `${name}: there is no outcome row ${i}` ``, `` `${name}: ${n} dice given
  for ${count}d${sides}` ``), the Zod issue sentences joined `; `, and at the
  HTTP edge `The request body is not JSON`.
- **HTTP:** `GET /api/v1/chats/{id}/custom-tools?action=interventions`;
  `POST /api/v1/chats/{id}/custom-tools?action=intervention-preview |
  intervention-reask | intervention-resolve` with the JSON bodies `{id,
  overrides}` / `{id, overrides}` / `{id, decision}`; an unknown action →
  the existing `Unknown action: <x>` envelope listing `availableActions`.
  Tauri: the same `Request`s through `invoke`.
- **Shapes** (key order as v4's `view.ts` / `interventions.ts` build them;
  absent optionals OMITTED, never `null`): `InterventionHoldView {id, tool,
  title, characterName: string|null, entrance: "llm"|"trigger",
  triggerPhrase?, createdAt: ms, eligible: {fields: string[], params:
  string[], effects: number[]}, hasCharacter, definition: {parameters,
  outcomes: [{when, state, message, brief?}], effects, revealOdds, hasLlm,
  diceSides?}, baseline: InterventionPreview, consults: [{ok, output,
  prompt}]}`; `InterventionPreview {run: InterventionRunView, staleAnswer,
  promptNow?, clampedParams: string[], warnings: string[]}`;
  `InterventionRunView {params, rollForm, notation?, raw, diceRolls?,
  diceBreakdown, rollSpan: RunSpan, value, valueSpan: RunSpan, outcomeIndex,
  tableOutcomeIndex?, state, message, chipLabel?, title, visibility,
  announcer: "pascal"|"character", llm?: {ok, output, prompt}, effects:
  InterventionEffectView[]}`; `InterventionEffectView {index, kind: "write"|
  "wardrobe"|"inventory"|"log", label, fired, skipped?, value?, items?,
  log?}`; `RunSpan {min, max, minExclusive?: true, maxExclusive?: true}`;
  `InterventionOverrides {params?: Record<string, number|string|boolean>,
  roll?, dice?: number[], value?, llmPrompt?, llmAnswer?: {ok, output},
  outcomeIndex?, message?, state?, chipLabel?, title?, visibility?,
  announcer?, effects?: Record<"<index>", EffectPin>}`; `EffectPin {mode:
  "auto"|"on"|"off", value?, items?: string[], log?}`; the decision `{kind:
  "deal", overrides} | {kind: "as-rolled"} | {kind: "veto"}`.
- **Event:** `RealtimeTopic::CustomToolInterventions` = `"customToolInterventions"`,
  scoped by chat id — the frame `{"v":1,"topic":"customToolInterventions",
  "id":"<chatId>","at":<server-ms>}` on hold add and on settle (two frames
  per hold; the bus coalesces within its window).
- **`pascalMeta.intervention`** on a dealt message (the badge's input), the
  LAST key of `pascalMeta`: `{resolvedAt: ISO, decision: "deal", changed:
  string[], dealt: {params, raw, diceRolls?, value, outcomeIndex, state,
  message, chipLabel?, toolTitle, visibility, announcer, llm?: {ok, output,
  prompt}, effects?: [{index, fired, target, next?}]}, reasks?, staleAnswer?:
  true}`; absent when never held or let stand as rolled.
- **The run result on every wire that spreads it** (the chat POST `result`,
  the Workbench preview / `BenchRoll`): `…, llm?, chipLabel?, effects?, draw:
  {form:"range", unit} | {form:"dice", faces}, rollSpan, valueSpan,
  llmPromptRendered?, tableOutcomeIndex?`.
- **The Workbench library entry:** `intervention: boolean` after
  `triggerCount` (before P4.D343's `effect`).
- **The chat-settings key** `customToolIntervention?: boolean` (GET / PUT) —
  P4.D341 (row) + P4.D356 (PUT arm); the SPA's Automation row posts
  `{customToolIntervention: value}`.
- The guard `pascal_intervention_wire_contract.rs` pins the verbs, the
  variants, the topic string and the three error sentences so P4.D346
  authors blind.

  - **Node.** `PATH=$HOME/.nvm/versions/node/v24.13.1/bin:$PATH` (a REAL path
    in every env block).
  - **The sweep driver** is the sanctioned per-family path: `cd <the lane's
    worktree> && /opt/homebrew/bin/python3.13 harness/tools/recipe_sweep.py
    --run <family> --v4 "$PIN" --v5w <the lane's ABSOLUTE SPACELESS worktree
    path>`. **`--v5w` is the tree the regen WRITES INTO**, so it is the
    lane's own worktree (`~/source/quilltap-v5/.claude/worktrees/<orderid-
    lowercase>` — spaceless; memory note `sweep-driver-spaced-path`), NEVER
    `main`'s tree. From a `.claude/` venue add `--force`; `--families` wants
    the FULL test-file stem, comma-separated. **Twenty lanes share ONE
    machine: never two sweeps at once; a lane announces a sweep to the
    human before starting it.** A family reporting `SKIP` in 0.00 s did not
    run. The system python 3.9 cannot parse the driver. The driver does
    NOT rewrite a recipe's `/tmp` outputs (memory note
    `parallel-lane-shared-resources` §3) — copy outputs to a lane-private
    dir right after a sweep.

  - **Shared `/tmp` paths.** Recipe headers write SHARED `/tmp` fixture

    hand with every output under `/tmp/<orderid-lowercase>/` (memory note
    `parallel-lane-shared-resources`). A jest recipe without `--testTimeout`
    gets `--testTimeout=180000`. Builders that re-mint ids per run: mask,
    never `ORDER BY` a minted id.

    rows AFTER `rowsLoaded++`, BEFORE the `CONSOLIDATED` branch; `isDemotable
    = hot && AUTO && !keystone`; `policyProtectionOf` answers `'keystone'`

    actually doomed (`if (!grew) break`) — port as shipped; `protection()`
    answers `{score: 1, protected: true, protectedBySource: true}`. In
    recall, "every digest is cut before any keystone" holds only INSIDE the
    inner circle (`innerCircleTrimOrder` sorts `Number(b.keystone) −
    Number(a.keystone)` first): present-character keystones are LIFTED into
    the circle at `tier −1` ahead of that character's digests, self
    keystones join the anchors, and `renderArchive` / `renderHead` render a
    keystone's `content` first (`summary` first otherwise). `KEYSTONES_PER_
    SUBJECT_MAX = 5`; the v4 quirk — `POST /memories` with `keystone: true`
    answers 400 `The memory was saved, but not as a keystone: <msg>` AFTER
    writing the row — is ported as the bytes (Q3). `keystone` appears in
    EVERY parsed Memory (between `consolidatedAt` and `createdAt`), so every
    byte pin of a memory row moves; `hydrateRawRows` coerces number / bigint
    / null to a boolean and DROPS a string cell with the WARN.
  - (i) **The WCAG ownership rule** (wcag survey §7.3): "the lane with the
    larger or structural hunk owns the file; the a11y lane records a HANDOFF
    naming the rule(s) R1–R12 and v4's bytes; the owner applies them in its
    own commit; the unifier checks the handoff list. Ties go to the feature
    lane (the a11y edits are mechanical)." Every sibling SPA lane applies
    rules **R1–R12** (the binding text is Shared contract C9) to the files it
    owns and records each as a `HANDOFF:` for P4.D358's census; P4.D358's
    static census script takes an allowlist for sibling-owned files, which
    the unifier empties. Already CONVERGED (not reds): the sidebar switches,
    `Allow Any Character`, the edit box, the composer `Message`, the
    slide-over's focus. The real gap: of 34 overlay components, 13 have no
    `role="dialog"`; `qt-modal` (77 hosts) carries `role` + `aria-modal` +
    the close label but NO `aria-labelledby` / `tabindex` / focus / Escape.

- **§R.5 — Log lines.** Every line this round ports or moves lands with a
  capture pin (the thread-scoped rig) AND its silence leg.
  - A line emitted INSIDE a `Db::write` closure runs on the writer thread and
    is INVISIBLE to the rig: run the home on the caller thread (memory note
    `capture-rig-writer-thread`) or use `global_capture`.
  - A `db::fallback` line renders the BARE SQLite / Zod message
    (`error_text`), never `sqlite error: …`.
  - A structured v4 field that is an array or object renders through the
    `…Json` file-layer convention; v4's camelCase keys are the keys; an
    `undefined` v4 field is OMITTED; child-logger bindings are fields on the
    line; `durationMs` and other wall-clock fields are MASKED.
  - Open before `captured_with`; a v4 catch UNREACHABLE through v4's real
    code is pinned NEGATIVE with a v5 fallback home.
  - **Migration lines follow the standing precedent** (P4.D249 / P4.D304 /
    P4.D315 / P4.D324, `host.rs`): v4's migration INFO / ERROR texts are
    NO-PORT and each boot ensure logs ONE v5-worded INFO per added column /
    table / index.

- **§R.7 — Red-first.**
  - **Measure red before touching source** — on unported `main` for the
    wave-0 lanes, on the `KEYSTONE` tip for its dependents (a contract
    refusal arm answering `not_available` IS the red). The lane record
    carries the red count per family. A test that passes before the fix is
    a test of nothing.
  - **Ruled divergences** are NAMED both-ways tables in the idiom of
    `FRESH_STORE_RESIDUAL` / `BOOT_CREATED_HISTORY_TABLE`, so a v4
    convergence trips them. This round's planned names: **P4.D345**
    `HOLD_KEEP_ALIVE_NO_STREAM` (v4's 15 s keep-alive ping — no v5
    counterpart by architecture), `ROUND_DROPS_MIN_EXCLUSIVE` (only if the
    corpus shows it); **P4.D354** `REFUSING_SEAM_LEAVES_PDF_UNEXTRACTED`
    (the backfill and the `_indexed` bridges leave a PDF / DOCX row at
    `'none'` while `DocumentTextExtractor::is_available()` is `false`; the
    REST path keeps v4's `failed` bytes); **P4.D347** `ECHO_SLICE_SURROGATE`
    + `SENTENCE_SLICE_SURROGATE` (a JS `slice` that cuts a surrogate pair —
    v5 rounds to the code-point boundary); **P4.D343** the dry scope's
    table-delta proof (whatever the `DryBuildContextSeams` wrapper does not
    cover is a recorded divergence, never a silent write) and `plugin_tool_
    effects` as NO-COUNTERPART; **P4.D350** `PUBLISH_AFTER_COMMIT` only if
    the human overrules its R-1 (v4 publishes at the write; v5 after the
    commit); **P4.D355** the explicit `terminal_sessions` DELETE on chat
    delete (v5's cascade stand-in on a generateDDL table with no FK — named
    in its record; R-R); **P4.D341** none owed (Q3's post-write 400 is
    ported as bytes); **P4.D352** an ADAPTER divergence recorded in the SPA
    (ProseMirror `Decoration.inline` for v4's CSS Custom Highlight API; the
    four `::highlight(name)` rules become class rules with the same tokens);
    **P4.D353** a Tier-3 code-block note only if R-B is overruled to (C).

- **§R.8 — The unifier's accumulation.** Each lane's record states its
  `Cargo.toml` versions at close; the unifier takes the HIGHEST and bumps +1
  for the union commit; `Cargo.lock` follows. The SPA: the highest of the
  SPA lanes, +1. CHANGELOG entries and `status-log.md` lane records
  union-merge (append-only).
- **§R.9 — One machine.**
  - **Workspace gates:** at most TWO Rust workspace gates at once. Count them
    with ONE `ps -ax -o pid,etime,command | grep "cargo test --workspace" |
    grep -v grep`; if two run, queue yours in ONE background command waiting
    on the siblings' PIDs (`while kill -0 <pidA> && kill -0 <pidB>; do sleep
    20; done; sleep $((RANDOM % 20)); <re-count once; if still two, wait on
    those PIDs>; cargo test …`), never on a pattern (memory notes
    `no-pattern-kill-on-shared-machine`, `parallel-lane-shared-resources`
    §2 — co-waiters all fire on one exit; re-count after the wait).
  - **Sweeps and Playwright:** one sweep at a time (announced); one
    Playwright run at a time (lanes by file only; the full suite is the
    unifier's). New own-instance e2e files' server ports are unique per
    lane (4319–4340, 4343–4350, 4352 and mocks 45301–45304, 45340–45348 are
    taken): **P4.D344 4353 / mock 45351**, **P4.D346 4354 / mock 45352**,
    **P4.D352 4355 / mock 45353**, **P4.D353 4356 / mock 45354**, **P4.D358
    4357 / mock 45355**, **P4.D359 4358 / mock 45356**, **P4.D355 4359 /
    mock 45357** (only if a terminal e2e is added). A lane that only edits
    an EXISTING spec takes no port.
  - **Long jobs:** `run_in_background: true`, full log in a lane-private
    file, awaited by the completion notification — NEVER a `pgrep`/`grep`
    poll loop (CLAUDE.md), never `tail -N` on a gate log. A gate reads its
    oracles and source at RUN time: no regen and no source edit while one
    runs (memory note `lane-gate-hygiene`).
  - **Targets:** clone main's `target/` before the first build (`cp -cR`;
    `/carryout` rule 8 — same volume only, memory note
    `apfs-clone-same-volume-only`); main's `target/` was warmed by the
    `/setupphase` chain on 2026-10-29 at the orders' commit (the test /
    clippy set the lanes clone; the release bins were swept — an
    own-instance e2e that prefers `target/release/*` must delete the stale
    release bins from its clone or build release, memory note
    `lane-spa-env-node-modules-and-release-bins`); a stacked lane may clone
    its parent lane's worktree target instead, when that lane is not
    mid-build.
  - **`apps/web/dist/`:** a fresh worktree has none — build it before any
    workspace clippy / test. **`apps/web/node_modules`:** `npm ci` in the
    lane (a cloned copy fails every vitest spec with NG0203).
  - **Concurrency:** the cap is the two-gate rule, not the lane count;
    twenty lanes do NOT all run at once (§S.0 waves). Disk at planning:
    144 GiB free on the repo volume with `target/` at 347 GB BEFORE the
    warm sweep — the human sizes the waves from the post-sweep numbers in
    the hand-off report.

- **§R.11 — Rulings for the human.** Each order's "Rulings made at
  planning" are the planner's calls; the human may overrule any before lane
  start (an overruled ruling is a STOP item for that lane). The round-level
  ones to read FIRST — **APPROVED AS PLANNED by the human at the hand-off
  (2026-10-29): the npm-cache step (ONE online `npm ci` in a pin worktree
  before lane start), the pin `236c04e35`, R-B option A (the four diagram
  deps), the three SPA convergences (R-N), the `@axe-core/playwright` dev
  dep, and every other planner call below stands unless a later note here
  says otherwise. Lanes do NOT start until the human says so.**
  - ⚖ **THE NPM CACHE GAP (vendor survey §F.4 — a PRE-ROUND BLOCKER):** the
    human's live checkout is at `4.12.0-dev.5` (`openai` 7.45.1, the v13
    driver), so the npm cache never saw the pin's versions — 44 of the 188
    tarballs the pin's lock adds / moves are `ENOTCACHED`; `npm ci --offline`
    at the pin FAILS as written. **The planner's call: ONE online `npm ci
    --no-audit --no-fund` in a pin worktree before any lane starts** (the
    human, or the first lane with the human's go-ahead), which populates the
    cache for every lane's `--offline` rebuild; record the resulting
    `node_modules/.package-lock.json` stamp. The alternative (the live
    `node_modules` symlink) runs every oracle on 7.45.1 / 1.5.21 / the v13
    driver and is REFUSED by this round. Every order's pin recipe says:
    verify `npm ci --offline` succeeds; if `ENOTCACHED`, STOP and report.
  - **THE PIN (human, 2026-10-09, standing)** — §R.1 / §R.2: `236c04e35`,
    v4's 4.11.0 code, NOT v4 HEAD at planning (the planner's call: the two
    newer commits carry an unruled driver / SQLite move; a released
    baseline is nameable; both go to the next round with the ruling).
  - **R-A — ONE `fresh_schema.json` re-dump at the pin, owned by the
    keystone (P4.D341)**, carrying the SEVENTEENTH to TWENTY-FIRST moves;
    no other lane re-dumps; `migration_indexes.json` moves ONCE in the same
    commit (`idx_memories_keystone`); every ensure's stamping decision is
    §R.4 (b)'s. The same commit carries the ONE `schema-key-order.json` dump
    and the ONE `qtap-export.schema.json` re-vendor; the custom-tool schema
    is P4.D345's; the tool catalog is P4.D350's `CATALOG`; the definition
    corpus is P4.D345's. **All five ensures UNSTAMPED**; the keystone ensure
    creates the partial index itself; the llm-logs column ensure runs BEFORE
    the structural verify; `storyteller_rounds` is created on a pre-round
    instance with the MIGRATION's DDL verbatim + both indexes (ruling Q2 —
    the human may overrule).
  - ⚖ **R-B — the four diagram client dependencies** (`mermaid` 12.2.0,
    `@hpcc-js/wasm-graphviz` 1.30.0, `svg2roughjs` 3.2.3, `dompurify`
    3.4.17 — the first client runtime deps since ProseMirror / KaTeX):
    measured — `mermaid@12.2.0` (122 MB unpacked, 23 deps incl. `d3`,
    `cytoscape`, `elkjs`, `katex ^0.18.0` — compatible with v5's pinned
    0.18.4; lazy-loaded, so the initial bundle is unaffected, but `npm ci`
    and the lockfile grow by hundreds of packages), `@hpcc-js/wasm-graphviz@
    1.30.0` (2.0 MB, zero deps, wasm inlined), `svg2roughjs@3.2.3` (1.9 MB),
    `dompurify@3.4.17` (1.6 MB; also mermaid's transitive); all four load by
    dynamic `import()` in v4 → Angular's esbuild builder emits lazy chunks
    OUTSIDE the `initial` budget (`angular.json:39-44`); wasm inlined — no
    `.wasm` fetch (`static_serve.rs:34` maps `application/wasm` anyway);
    Tauri's CSP is `null` (`tauri.conf.json:19`) and `quilltap-web` sets no
    page CSP, so no `'wasm-unsafe-eval'`. Options: **(A) port whole** with the
    four (v4 parity; the first `ng build` is the proof that esbuild splits
    `import('mermaid')`); **(B) mermaid + DOMPurify only** — saves 3.9 MB of
    lazy weight and two deps, loses `dot` / `graphviz` / `svg` fences (shown
    as code with a loud note) and the hand-drawn Graphviz; **(C) defer

- **§R.14 — Stacking rules.** Each named stacking commit — `KEYSTONE`
  (P4.D341), `CONTEXT` (P4.D347), `SPINE` (P4.D348), `CATALOG` (P4.D350's
  FIRST commit), `FILES` (P4.D354) — is ONE commit whose subject starts with
  its name; the owner records the sha in its status header ON ITS LANE
  BRANCH the moment it lands. A dependent lane — P4.D347 / P4.D342 / P4.D351
  / P4.D356 from `KEYSTONE`; P4.D348 from `CONTEXT`; P4.D343 / P4.D345 from
  `SPINE` (which is on `CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`); P4.D349 from `CATALOG`;
  P4.D355 from `FILES` — finds the sha per `/carryout` rule 2 (`git log --all
  --format='%h %s' | grep -E '^[0-9a-f]+ <NAME>:'`, confirmed against the
  parent's lane-branch copy of the order) and branches from it — never from
  `main`, never a guessed sha; if the parent has not recorded it, STOP and
  report. A stacked lane may PRE-START from `main` on work that calls none
  of its parent's contract names — P4.D342's prompt bytes (the four
  extraction ladders + the consolidation tail) + the inner-circle resolver
  (`services/inner_circle.rs`, the tuning knobs); P4.D343's `tools/effects.rs`
  + `pascal/tool_effect.rs` + `StreamParams.tool_choice` + the `tool-build`
  re-aim (its unit E) + the pure `storyteller/*` modules; P4.D345's hold
  registry + eligibility / overrides / recompute / spans + the engine's
  `ReplayInputs` + the ONE corpus regen + the schema re-vendor; P4.D351's
  `services/annotations.rs` + `template_prompt_hint.rs` ports; P4.D356's
  NanoGPT half + the Tier R verify; P4.D349's `pronoun-presets` /
  `pronoun-guidance` ports + the image-prompt `humanoid` threading; P4.D355's
  `db/log_entries.rs` + the terminal host; P4.D348's `operator_post.rs` /
  `whisper_send.rs` / `turn_control.rs` additions and the funnel recorder;
  P4.D347 has no pre-start (its first unit widens `RawMessage`, which rides
  the keystone's row shapes) — rebasing onto the parent's commit before the
  first commit that does call a contract name; that rebase is the one
  sanctioned exception to "no rebase mid-lane". Two sibling-stacked parts
  (not lane-named commits): P4.D346's grammar-twin part (`pascal/custom-tool-
  types.ts` + the corpus copy + the spec header) stacks on P4.D345's Tier-1
  commit, or authors blind and lets the unifier run `custom-tool-types.
  corpus.spec.ts` — the order states which; P4.D359's chips stack on
  P4.D353's Tier-1 commit (the `message-content.ts` hook). Every other lane
  cuts from `main` at the orders' commit.

## §S — Unifier wires (what no single lane can do)

- **§S.0 — Waves (the recommended execution; the two-gate cap rules the
  machine, not the lane count).**
  - **Wave 0, from the orders' commit, at once:** P4.D341 (→ `KEYSTONE`,
    the Opus / Fable tier), P4.D350 (→ `CATALOG` as its FIRST commit, then
    its own units), P4.D354 (→ `FILES`), and in parallel P4.D360, P4.D357,
    P4.D358. That is six worktrees.
  - **Wave 1, as slots free (still from the orders' commit):** the five SPA
    feature lanes P4.D344, P4.D346, P4.D352, P4.D353, P4.D359 (each authors
    against the contracts with gated beats; P4.D346's grammar-twin part and
    P4.D359's chips wait for their sibling's Tier-1 commit).
  - **Wave A, from `KEYSTONE`:** P4.D347 (→ `CONTEXT`) FIRST, then P4.D342,
    P4.D351, P4.D356 (its NanoGPT + Tier R half may have PRE-STARTED from
    `main`). **From `CATALOG`:** P4.D349 — FIRST among the independents
    (R-H). **From `FILES`:** P4.D355.
  - **Wave B, from `CONTEXT`:** P4.D348 (→ `SPINE`).
  - **Wave C, from `SPINE`:** P4.D343 and P4.D345 (the two turn-side
    consumers every later union depends on).

  Within a wave every lane compiles against the C sections below it alone.
- **§S.1 — The pick order.** P4.D341 → P4.D350 → P4.D349 → P4.D347 →
  P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 → P4.D342 → P4.D351 → P4.D356 → P4.D354 →
  P4.D355 → P4.D357 → P4.D344 → P4.D346 → P4.D352 → P4.D353 → P4.D359 →
  P4.D360 → P4.D358. The reasons per adjacency: `KEYSTONE` first (every
  server lane's row shapes, the dumps, the ensures); P4.D350 next because
  `CATALOG` is the base P4.D349 branches from, and P4.D349 right after it
  (R-H — the three LIVE-DATA erasure writers land before anything else
  touches a vault); P4.D347 → P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 in their stacking
  order (`CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`, `SPINE` on `CONTEXT`, the two consumers of
  S1 / S2 / C1 / `TurnStopReach` on `SPINE`; P4.D343 before P4.D345 so
  `trigger_runner.rs`'s pre-run ceiling block precedes the post-run `Vetoed`
  arm and `realtime/types.rs` lands `storytellerRounds` NINTH before
  `customToolInterventions` TENTH); P4.D342 and P4.D351 (keystone-only
  consumers, no spine seam) after the spine chain; P4.D356 after P4.D345 so
  the `customToolIntervention` arm it writes for P4.D345 and the
  `punctuationWarnings` key it writes for P4.D352 are in the tree when their
  families re-run, and after P4.D351 so `settings-routes` is regenerated
  ONCE at the end of the server picks; P4.D354 → P4.D355 (`FILES` then the
  two bridge sites switched); P4.D357 before every SPA lane (the CSS lines
  the fenced appends sit beside); P4.D344 before P4.D346 (`_index.css`
  imports in v4's order; `RealtimeTopicAppend`'s SPA half NINTH then TENTH;
  `LibraryEntryKeys`' SPA badges in v4's order); P4.D352 before P4.D353
  (`renderMarkdownToHtml` is P4.D352's; P4.D353 post-passes its HTML);
  P4.D353 before P4.D359 (P4.D359's chips stack on P4.D353's `message-
  content.ts` hook; its `_variables.css` append sits after P4.D353's);
  P4.D360 LATE (its help / docs guards judge a union carrying every server
  feature; its schema-guard CHECK sees the final constants; the P4.D339
  precedent: fourteenth) — nineteenth here, before P4.D358; P4.D358 LAST
  (its census allowlist for sibling-owned HANDOFF files is emptied against
  the finished union; its toast-selector first commit is cherry-picked
  EARLIER by any sibling that edits one of the 20 spec files).

  free), then the full suite. A red is root-caused against the server logs
  before it is accepted as the recorded timing class.

## Shared contracts (binding; name-level — the long forms are the surveys' §7 and, where an order is finished, that order's contract section)

- **C1 — the KEYSTONE surface P4.D341 lands at `KEYSTONE`** (keystone
  survey §7.3's K / C-1 / C-2 / C-3 / C-4; P4.D341 writes the FINAL

- **C9 — the WCAG rules R1–R12 (P4.D358 writes; EVERY SPA lane applies to
  the files it owns and records a HANDOFF for the census):**
  - **R1 Modal dialog surface** (`dff76ebef`): the dialog CARD (never the
    overlay) carries `role="dialog"` — `role="alertdialog"` where the
    dialog-roles table says so — `aria-modal="true"`, `aria-labelledby=
    "<titleId>"` with `id="<titleId>"` on the visible title element (a
    per-instance id: `inject(UniqueId)` if the SPA has one, else a module
    counter `qt-dialog-<n>`); `tabindex="-1"`; the shared focus directive
    (`qtDialogFocus`, P4.D358's `ui/dialog-focus.ts`) for focus-in / Tab-trap
    / restore; Escape closes unless the host's busy gate says otherwise (keep
    v4's gate expression). For a `qt-modal` host: nothing — the primitive
    does it.
  - **R2 Non-modal floating surface** (`FloatingDialog`): `role="dialog"
    aria-modal="false" aria-labelledby`, `tabindex="-1"`, focus in on open
    and back to the opener on close, NO Tab trap, close button `aria-label=
    "Close"`, Escape inside closes it (topmost: `stopPropagation`), Escape
    elsewhere closes it only while `!document.querySelector('[aria-modal=
    "true"]')`, both skipping `defaultPrevented`.
  - **R3 Icon-only buttons** carry v4's `aria-label` byte: `Close`, `Cancel`,
    `Dismiss message`, `Close gallery`, `` Remove alias ${alias} ``, `Up one
    folder`, `Toggle model list` (+ `aria-expanded`).
  - **R4 Visible label ↔ control:** `<label for="<id>">` + `id="<id>"` on the
    control; never a bare `<span>` caption beside a control.
  - **R5 Heading-captioned control:** `aria-labelledby="<headingId>"` with
    `id` on the heading (ProfilesTab / ImageGenerationCard / ModelBehaviorCard
    / FieldSelectionStep / SuggestionCard / ContextCompression switch).
  - **R6 Placeholder-only / caption-less control:** `aria-label` with v4's
    string (never invent a string; where v4 has none, do not add one).
  - **R7 Switch:** `role="switch"` + `aria-checked` + a name (`aria-label` or
    `aria-labelledby`); the Prospero one is `type="button"` too.
  - **R8 Clickable non-button:** `role="button" tabindex="0" (keydown)=
    "activateOnKey($event)"` (P4.D358's `ui/activate-on-key.ts`) with
    `aria-expanded` (FileTable) / `aria-pressed` (gallery tile, quick-hide
    tags) where v4 sets them. Prefer a real `<button>` where the layout
    allows (v4 kept spans only inside `<tr>` / heading contexts and under
    the uppercase-button themes).
  - **R9 Live region for notices:** the toast container `role="status"
    aria-live="polite" data-toast-container`; an error toast `role="alert"`
    (P4.D358 only).
  - **R10 Colour-only state gets words:** `<span class="sr-only">Unmoderated
    chat.</span>`; ` (overheard)` appended to the whisper label.
  - **R11 Async result announced:** `<span class="sr-only" role="status"
    aria-live="polite">…</span>` (DocumentPane: `editingIndicator ??
    (saveLabel === 'Saved' ? 'Saved' : '')`).
  - **R12 Pointer targets ≥ 24 px** — CSS, P4.D357 (`.qt-tab-close`);
    templates: `min-w-6 min-h-6` on the inline copy-id button (P4.D358).
  The `e2e/support/toasts.ts` helper: `export const toastStack = (page:
  Page) => page.locator('[data-toast-container] > div')` and `toastByClass

## Ownership (binding; identical table in every order of this round)

`docs/CHANGELOG.md` and `docs/developer/porting/status-log.md` are
append-only for EVERY lane (the unifier union-merges them); `drift-ledger.md`
is `/unify`'s. "Stage" names the base: `main` at the orders' commit, or the
named stacking commit.

| Order | Stage | Owns (files / regions) | Must NOT touch |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P4.D341** KEYSTONE | `main` → `KEYSTONE` | §R.10 (o) whole (the five dumps / re-vendors); NEW `db/{memory_keystone_repair,storyteller_mode_repair,llm_logs_storyteller_round_id_repair,roleplay_templates_unmarked_repair}.rs` + the middle step of `db/chat_settings_4_11_repair.rs` + `db/mod.rs`;
